## Cross-cutting findings

These are properties of the enforcement architecture rather than of any single artifact.

| # | Finding | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| X-01 | The UEC gate reports OPEN while every ratcheted defect class sits at a non-zero ceiling. OPEN means 'no class grew', not 'no class exists'. | `--json` verdict OPEN; ratchet engines_with_no_invoker=6, engines_without_a_test=18, artifacts_with_one_invocation_plane=16, declarations_no_code_consumes=3 |
| X-02 | No execution history is inside Repository Truth. All four evidence locations are gitignored, so a fresh clone contains zero proof that any gate ever ran. | .gitignore excludes `.ucos/`, `.ucos-verification-evidence/`, `determinism-evidence/`, `.runtime/`; `git ls-files` over all four returns nothing |
| X-03 | The single certification-currency mechanism in the repository is re-render-and-diff, and it covers only 12 of 32 workflows. The other 20 never re-derive the artifact they certify. | `git diff --exit-code` present in 12 workflow files; absent in the remaining 20 |
| X-04 | Requirement coverage cannot fail a build. The only requirement-plane verdict steps are marked continue-on-error, by explicit decision recorded in the workflow. | .github/workflows/closure009-gate.yml — 2 steps with `continue-on-error: true` (`--gate` and `--baseline-gate`); the file states assimilation is too low for them to block |
| X-05 | All 12 baseline preconditions of the requirement plane FAIL, and `baseline_permitted=false`. The gate that would refuse on this is the one made non-blocking in X-04. | 00-MASTER/UAKOS-CLOSURE-009/requirements.json — baseline_conditions BC-01..BC-12 all verdict=FAIL; baseline_permitted=false |
| X-06 | Zero of 549 requirements reach all 9 traceability tiers; 314 have implementation, 216 have a test, 241 have certification. | 00-MASTER/UAKOS-CLOSURE-009/requirements.json traceability_tiers; 07-CONSTITUTIONAL-TRACEABILITY-MATRIX.md records the 70/549 full-chain figure |
| X-07 | The requirement model has no gap class for 'requirement without a test' or 'requirement without a gate'. Those sets are derivable from the spine but carry no owner and no required action, so nothing is accountable for closing them. | requirements.json gap_classes RG-A01..RG-E03 — no NO-TEST and no NO-GATE class; the derivable set is 333 requirements |
| X-08 | 96 of 204 machine-readable law records declare no `check` at all: their text is computed by nothing. A further 103 declare no `blocking` field, so a violation cannot be graded. | Law records with no check: cios-bindings.json 24, uis-declaration.json 20, uis.json 20, urr-declaration.json#inference_laws 13, ucef-framework.json 12, uga-declaration.json#alignment_invariants 7 |
| X-09 | The UIS law set exists in two independent copies of 20 laws each. Neither is derived from the other, so they can diverge silently. | 00-MASTER/UIS-001/uis-declaration.json (20 laws) and 00-MASTER/UIS-001/uis.json (20 laws), both carrying UIL-11 blocking=false |
| X-10 | One constitutional authority chain terminates in a document no code reads: `ucon-declaration.json` names `ucxi-declaration.json` as its superior, and `ucxi-declaration.json` has zero code consumers. | engine/enforcement_closure/declaration.py docstring; consumers measured as 0 for ceu-declaration.json, urr-declaration.json, ucxi-declaration.json |
| X-11 | Certification identity existence is proven; certification identity *sensitivity* is not. 5 of 20 governed declarations expose the uniform interface UEC-L-13 mutation-tests; for the other 15 a semantic edit is not shown to move the digest. | UEC ratchet declarations_with_uniform_identity=5; engine/enforcement_closure/contract.py records the measured UCON case where flipping `blocking` on UCON-L-01 left declaration_digest byte-identical at 192c63af |
| X-12 | Every one of the 32 workflows fires on push and pull_request to every branch. There is no dispatch-only and no scheduled workflow. Trigger coverage is not where the enforcement holes are — execution binding is. | 0 workflows with workflow_dispatch, 0 with schedule, 32 with both push and pull_request |
| X-13 | 144 of 146 persisted certification objects carry a digest, but only 2 has a producer on an automatic execution path. The repository mints certification identity at scale and re-derives almost none of it, so a committed certificate can drift from the state it certifies with no signal. | Measured per object in ENFORCEMENT-CLOSURE-MATRIX.md §J; the Makefile itself records the failure mode at lines 306-314, describing a certificate that went stale across 142 commits and 500 files while still presenting as valid |
| X-14 | Only one persisted certification object in the repository declares a `producer` field. For every other object, the producing module must be inferred from directory ancestry rather than read from the object, so provenance is a convention rather than a recorded fact. | `producer` present in 1 of 146 objects (00-MASTER/UCOS-UGA-001/07-CERTIFICATION.json → 00-MASTER/UCOS-UGA-001/uga_engine.py) |
| X-15 | **A live mutant that disables a law is present in the working tree, and the gate verdict is unchanged by it.** `engine/enforcement_closure/contract.py` currently has `certification_identity_exists` (UEC-L-08) replaced by `return []  # MUTANT`. The gate still reports OPEN and UEC-L-08 still reports HOLDS. The mutant SURVIVES because the class it measures is empty at this state (0 offenders against a ceiling of 0), so a working UEC-L-08 and a deleted UEC-L-08 are observationally identical. The law cannot currently detect its own disablement. | `git diff engine/enforcement_closure/contract.py` shows line 367 changed from `_ratcheted(probe, "declarations_without_a_certification_identity")` to `return []`. The mutant is working-tree only — absent from HEAD. The intact underlying measurement `declarations_without_a_certification_identity(probe)` returns [] independently, confirming the surviving-mutant diagnosis rather than a masked violation. |
| X-16 | The measured working tree is not HEAD. A substantial body of uncommitted work is present, including whole engine packages staged but not committed. Any gate verdict quoted here is a property of this working tree, not of the committed repository. | `git status --porcelain` reports 91 staged or modified paths plus 3 untracked at measurement time, among them the entire `engine/construct/`, `engine/enforcement_closure/` and `engine/recursive_knowledge/` packages and their declarations |

## Corrections to earlier estimates made during this measurement

| Claim | Status | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| 33 laws declare a `check` with no implementing predicate | **WITHDRAWN — false positive.** | The detector searched for `def <check>`; the repository convention is `def check_<name>` bound through a `LAW_CHECKS` registry. Re-measured: 108 of 108 declared checks are implemented, 0 unimplemented. |
| 282 dead path references across 69 registers, including a vanished `tools/` tree | **CORRECTED to 53 references across 4 registers.** | The `tools/` cluster was a prefix-stripping artefact: the registers reference `00-BOOK/tools/ukb.py`, which exists. Verified present at 00-BOOK/tools/ukb.py. |
| 23 Makefile gate targets are unreachable from CI | **REFINED to 15.** | A target name absent from CI does not mean its enforcement is unexecuted — 8 targets wrap an engine that CI invokes directly. Re-measured by resolving each recipe to its engine, module or shell script and testing that for CI and verify.sh reachability. |
| Certification objects are produced by code wherever a module names their filename | **REJECTED — false positive at scale.** | Matching a basename anywhere reported 142 unrelated `*_realize.py` modules as the producer of every `certification.json`-style object, because that basename is a common literal. Re-measured requiring the naming module to sit in the artifact's own directory or an ancestor of it, which is how these files are actually written (`os.path.join(HERE, "07-CERTIFICATION.json")`). |
| Certification objects carry no digest unless a top-level digest key is present | **CORRECTED from 2 to 144 of 146.** | The first pass checked only top-level keys from a fixed list and missed nested proof blocks — 00-MASTER/UCOS-UGA-001/07-CERTIFICATION.json carries existence_digest, identity_digest, registry_digest, graph_digest and invariant_digest inside `proof`. Re-measured over all depths and any `*_digest`/`*_hash`/`*_sha256` key. This correction moved the finding from 'no identity' to the sharper 'identity exists and nothing re-derives it' (X-13). |
| Laws are executed wherever their declaration sits on an automatic path | **CORRECTED — the predicate was incoherent.** | The first pass reported 121 laws Executed against 108 Reachable, i.e. laws executing without an implementing predicate. `Executed` now requires `Reachable`, giving 94. |

## What this document does not do

It fixes nothing. No declaration was edited, no ceiling was moved, no artifact was withdrawn, no gate was wired. Every finding above is left standing exactly as measured.
